- **evidence-governance**: 開発プロセス全般（設計、実装、テスト、CI/CD統合）における品質・セキュリティ・ガバナンス監査証跡（Evidence）の都度管理。ADR、5者レビュー、テストログ、ライセンスチェック、日本語化遵守状況の収集とドキュメント化に適用します。 ([sh0jitmy/Musubi](https://github.com/sh0jitmy/Musubi/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evidence-governance))
- **evidence-grade**: Use when writing or reviewing any user-facing recommendation, training directive, methodology value (success-rate target, FSRS interval, difficulty offset, daily volume, per-band rule), or research claim for the chess-training app. Attaches an evidence grade (A/B/C/D), a citation, and a "why this / why now" rationale, and prevents overstating evidence. ([kleinebossie/Mainline](https://github.com/kleinebossie/Mainline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evidence-grade) · ★10)
- **evidence-grade**: Use when grading run evidence or deciding which claims a result can support. ([CrazyShout/MORA](https://github.com/CrazyShout/MORA/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-grade) · ★1)
- **evidence-grade**: Grade evidence quality — source credibility, recency, methodology, reproducibility, relevance ([hummbl-io/agent-skills](https://github.com/hummbl-io/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-grade))
- **evidence-grader**: Grades evidence strength for SourceRecord items by primary-source status, peer review, freshness, independence and confidence. Use before synthesis in research workflows. ([xninetzy-labs/xninetzy](https://github.com/xninetzy-labs/xninetzy/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/evidence-grader) · ★5)
- **evidence-grades**: Use when the reply includes facts, numbers, status, or done/not-done judgments that must separate verified claims from guesses. ([obqyuxi/biz-agent-guardrails](https://github.com/obqyuxi/biz-agent-guardrails/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-grades))
- **evidence-grading**: Grade the evidence behind a claim before betting on it — the hierarchy for business evidence (experiments &gt; usage data &gt; surveys &gt; interviews &gt; anecdotes &gt; opinion), the fit-for-decision test, and the mixed-evidence verdicts that real questions produce. Use when asked how strong is our evidence for this, grade what we know before the decision, is this enough to bet on, or we have three anecdotes and a survey — now what. Produces the evidence inventory with grades, the sufficiency verdict against the decision's stakes, and the cheapest-upgrade path. ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pm-cowork/skills/evidence-grading) · ★1.6k · 3 copies)
- **evidence-grading**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/evidence-grading) · ★745)
- **evidence-grading**: Assign A/B/C/D evidence grades to claims by walking source type, corroboration count, and procedural stage through a deterministic decision tree. Used by Stephen for the verification gate and by every researcher for self-grading before handoff. ([xiaolai/no-one-did-it](https://github.com/xiaolai/no-one-did-it/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evidence-grading) · ★92)
- **evidence-grading**: Grade the quality and strength of veterinary evidence using a veterinary-specific evidence hierarchy. Textbooks carry more weight in veterinary medicine than in human medicine. Use when evaluating the reliability of clinical information. ([OpenVet-Projects/VetClaw](https://github.com/OpenVet-Projects/VetClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/literature/evidence-grading) · ★50 · 2 copies)
- **evidence-grading** ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-healthcare/scopes/clinical-decision-support/skills/evidence-grading) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **evidence-grading**: Grade the evidence behind a claim before betting on it — the hierarchy for business evidence (experiments usage data surveys interviews anecdotes opinion), the fit-for-decision test, and the mixed-evidence verdicts that real questions produce. Use when asked how strong is our evidence for this, grade what we know before the decision, is this enough to bet on, or we have three anecdotes and a survey — now what. ([Slipcast-dev/codex-skills](https://github.com/Slipcast-dev/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-grading) · ★1)
- **evidence-grading**: Assess evidence quality using GRADE/SOE framework. Rates certainty level and identifies downgrade reasons. ([yogsoth-ai/deep-insight](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/deep-insight/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-grading) · ★1)
- **evidence-grading**: Grading evidence strength from evidence TYPE, never from wording. Load when the user says "how strong is this evidence", "can I trust this number", "grade this source", "likelihood ratio", or "is this claim actually supported". Persuasion words cap credibility, they never raise it. ([gannino/your-assistant](https://github.com/gannino/your-assistant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/analyst/evidence-grading))
- **evidence-grading**: - Preserve **one variable per experiment** when grading evidence; mixed interventions should be graded more conservatively. - **Record everything** about the grading rationale so downstream agents can see why a claim was marked strong, partial, or unsupported. - Keep the **experiment and code change linked** when a claim depends on a specific implementation or ablation. - **Verify before claiming* ([Iranb/ClawAutoResearch](https://github.com/Iranb/ClawAutoResearch/tree/HEAD/skills/reviewer/evidence-grading))
- **evidence-graph**: Use for questions about this project's own documents or knowledge that need repository evidence and file citations, before opening sources. A conditional instruction such as citing files for knowledge questions does not make a general-topic question project knowledge. Skip general concept explanations, ordinary coding, and edits to supplied text unless the user asks to ground that topic in this repository's documents. For an applicable first lookup, use one shell tool call to read this SKILL.md and then run its sibling scripts/ask.py --project-root . --read-only --evidence-units --compact-pro… ([JadeKim042386/evidence-graph](https://github.com/JadeKim042386/evidence-graph/tree/HEAD/evidence-graph/skills/evidence-graph) · ★2)
- **evidence-graph**: Defines the committed trace from product requirements through system specifications to public source exports for CodeHUD, its @wrtnlabs/evidence populations, citations, exclusions, stable anchors, and the review of the required triangle. Use before adding, moving, or reviewing a contract document, changing a public export's evidence JSDoc, or reshaping evidence.config.ts. ([samchon/codehud](https://github.com/samchon/codehud/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/evidence-graph))
- **evidence-graph-builder**: 트랙 B 분석 산출물(selected_topic, causal_gate, analysis, interpretation, diagnostics, variable_transformation_registry)을 단일 evidence_graph.json으로 합본하는 결정형 스킬. D5 SSOT 역할. C-U(upstream-check)가 단일 graph만 참조하도록 단순화하여 augmentor·5 renderer 입력 슬롯 부담을 줄인다. 사용자가 "evidence graph 만들어줘", "트랙 B 합본", "분석 산출물 통합"을 트리거. - **evidence-grounded-completion**: Verifies completion claims with evidence before declaring done. Use before stating "done", "complete", "fixed", "shipped", "ready", or any synonym. Use also before asserting a factual property of an artefact you have not read (a state assertion in research, assessment, planning, or review), not only at completion. Also use before ending a turn on a stated intention ("continuing with X", "next I will do X"), which is a claim about your own next action.
